Read and follow [EXECUTION_STRATEGY.md](EXECUTION_STRATEGY.md) before continuing, planning, implementing, reviewing, or verifying migration work. Include its link and relevant requirements in every agent handoff and continuation record.

Planning reference: master f7bc610a86bcad1c14b69dce65b1d804df1b7934, November 21, 2026. User requirements translated into a master Goal, orchestration Kickstart, 27 initial goal packets and execution record system.

## Subsequent checkpoints

Use the continuation template. Include branch/HEAD, working-tree state, owner, exact current checkpoint, implemented vs accepted GUI IDs, partial changes, commands/evidence and limits, revalidation requirements, blockers/dependencies and exact next action. Keep historical entries factual and concise; do not overwrite the only resume state with a vague “continue migration.”

### G14-C automatic accepted Regex history

Both frontends share the exact accepted pattern and flags policy. A current successful nonempty pair is recorded once until the attempted pair changes or the user explicitly retries. ### G14-C explicit protected Regex clipboard

The explicit actions use one protected clipboard lane across the retained Regex workspace. Import replaces only Test Text, preserves the full oversized source and keeps a bounded preview and Clear. Copy uses complete current pattern, match, exact selected capture, displayed matches, match information, replacement result, or a counts-only case summary. An empty matched capture is copyable and an unmatched capture is unavailable. Both frontends use the same match and case-summary formatting.

Each operation checks its exact mount and relevant state revision. Case-result publication revokes an older summary without invalidating raw Pattern or Test Text import. Selected-match and selected-capture effects have separate authority. The Windows writer checks the live receipt after clipboard acquisition and before setting text. Pane Close, rehost, Quit and restart wait while an accepted operation owns the mount. Completion clears only the clipboard wait status, and entering or rendering the workspace does not access the clipboard.

### G13 checked Clipboard History Save, Copy and Remove

Save persists the exact nonblank draft. Remove stages an inline confirmation for the exact selected occurrence. Both compare the complete ordered disk snapshot and recheck native authority at each atomic replacement attempt. Explicit Copy compares the same snapshot and writes the full row through the guarded clipboard backend. On Windows an entry containing a NUL is refused before clipboard acquisition because native text cannot round-trip it.

The accepted effect owns one lane through acknowledgment, and Close, Quit and restart wait for it. An active dialog refuses new effects. A conflict retires the old row authority while preserving the draft or decision. A transient unreadable result can retry if the reread confirms the same version and complete order. Fresh activation is required for Save, Copy and confirmation. Cancel has no disk or clipboard effect. Focus returns to the exact pane when it still owns the foreground.

### G13 Clipboard Modify checked clipboard effects

Docked Modify offers Apply, Apply and Close, and Copy Result for an exact completed preview. An edited source remains eligible if initial Load failed. The worker uses the complete output and the stock loaded baseline. Apply requests review when the ordinary clipboard differs from that baseline. Copy Result follows its own fresh protected preflight and leaves the pane open. A reviewed overwrite requires the exact reviewed text and Windows clipboard stamp. Even an identical-text replacement requires fresh approval. Successful writes alone install the complete replaced text in the shared Undo slot.

The shared service owns the bounded preflights and commit lane. Windows captures the stamp after reading text while the reader still holds the clipboard, accounting for delayed rendering. The guarded writer checks native format availability, the stamp and live pane authority while acquired and before calling the existing writer. Private, nontext, busy and unavailable outcomes retain their categories. A late clipboard or authority change prevents the write and preserves Undo. The stock egui effect paths still use the same service. [Windows clipboard sequence](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/winuser/nf-winuser-getclipboardsequencenumber) · [Windows format enumeration](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/winuser/nf-winuser-enumclipboardformats)

The pane owns the pending effect through acknowledgment, freezes its editors, and retires outstanding Reload authority. Close, Quit and restart wait for the exact operation or review. Fresh scoped keyboard activation reaches the real controls, while carried Enter and Space repeats cannot confirm a review. The exact review starts with Cancel focus when the pane still owns focus. Apply and Close honors its original native visibility receipt and restores Search only if the pane still owns focus.

### G13 Clipboard Modify typed Undo

The exact stock `cm undo` Search action uses the existing external effect coordinator and shared Clipboard Modify Undo slot. Search and command completion never touch clipboard I/O. The typed route works with the pane closed. Pane initialization, mutation and overwrite review gate Undo, and the launcher ticket gates concurrent pane effects. Admitting Undo revokes any older source read while preserving the current draft and preview.

One stamped protected preflight precedes the exact original-text restore. A private item, no text, busy or timed-out read, late clipboard replacement, lost authority or write failure preserves the record. A missing record returns without clipboard I/O. Only one successful restore consumes the slot.

Success keeps the query, results and cursor, skips History and Usage, shows the stock success notification, and requests Hide without consulting the ordinary run preference. The queued hide checks the exact root, foreground, visibility, presentation owner, Search generation and query through native transition. A newer hotkey or explicit Dock takes precedence. Refusal stays visible with redacted feedback. A concurrent request to open a new Modify pane receives a wait message so its first Load can run after Undo drains.

#### Compilation-aware verification receipt

The [execution strategy](EXECUTION_STRATEGY.md) update is `717968af8d36abff6d6a838a39e30fc12275254f`. Existing timing reports identify the warm critical path. Retained Snippets Edit rebuilt three application units while reusing 815. The library test took 1409.79 s and ran concurrently with the ordinary library. Cargo attributed 810.88 s of the ordinary library's 1084.01 s to the frontend and 273.13 s to the remaining code generation interval. The launcher test started after that library. Management and Direct Execute also rebuilt three application units with the library test on their critical paths. Snippets Add and Remove reused 816 units and rebuilt only the launcher test in a 20.05 s build. The reports use two job slots on eight logical CPUs and leave the specific test linker interval unmeasured. The existing `fast-dev` profile uses application line tables and disables dependency debug info.

The required named Edit build kept the warm target, two jobs, profile and toolchain. Cargo timing `cargo-timing-20261126T122449516Z-b747ed0349948c0a.html` recorded 1478.84 s on the critical library test unit, 1127.58 s for the ordinary library with 816.75 s in its frontend, and 154.97 s for the launcher test. The Windows sampler logged 732 valid samples to `target/g13-snippets-named-edit-performance.jsonl`. It observed MSVC `link.exe` 14.44.35207 for sampled spans of 22.2 s on the critical test and 38.3 s on the launcher test. Process durations are approximate at the sampling resolution.

System CPU averaged 11.0%. Concurrent rustc working sets reached 20.61 GiB, with 3166 MiB of available memory at the lowest sample. System page reads averaged about 143 per second and include mapped-file faults. The target-volume transfer-latency samples averaged 21.8 ms. These measurements expose substantial compiler and I/O time and leave the precise paging and compiler wait causes unresolved. The observed linker spans and existing low debug-info settings give limited scope for a linker or debug-level experiment. Keep the current `fast-dev` profile, job count, linker and incremental cache. Warm dependency reuse and the shared compilation boundary make grouping library work the evidenced throughput improvement. Compiler caching or selective crate extraction needs a concrete repeatable bottleneck and appropriate ownership before a controlled experiment.

Continue with one shared prompt contract for standalone Fill and Run and editor Preview, followed by launcher-only consumers where practical.
